1. WinGo analysis mein use hone wale mathematical methods
1. Frequency Analysis
Pichhle 50, 100, 500 ya 1,000 results mein har number kitni baar aaya, ye count karna.
Example: 0 = 8 baar, 1 = 12 baar, 2 = 9 baar.
Kaam: Number distribution aur unusual frequencies dekhna.
2. Probability & Percentage
Kisi category ki historical frequency ko percentage mein badalna.
Formula: 
Kaam: Big/Small, colour ya number ki historical frequency compare karna.
3. Moving Window / Recent Trend
Sirf latest 10, 20 ya 50 rounds ka analysis karke usse longer history se compare karna.
Kaam: Recent distribution mein badlav identify karna; agla result guarantee nahi karna.
Streak & Transition Analysis
Jaise BIG → BIG → SMALL, ya kitni baar BIG ke baad SMALL aaya.
Kaam: Result sequences ka pattern measure karna. Independent draws mein streak khud se reversal ki guarantee nahi hoti.
5. Conditional Probability / Markov Analysis
Historical data mein check karna ki pichhle result ya pichhle do results ke baad kaunsa result kitni baar aaya.
Kaam: Sequence dependence test karna; sirf correlation milna kaafi nahi, out-of-sample validation bhi chahiye.
6. Statistical Significance
Chi-square test, confidence intervals aur expected frequency se check karna ki observed pattern random variation se alag hai ya nahi.
Kaam: False patterns ko genuine evidence samajhne ki galti kam karna.
7. Backtesting & Accuracy Validation
Purane data par model test karna, lekin har prediction ke waqt sirf us samay available data use karna. Phir unseen results par test repeat karna.
Kaam: Pata karna ki method simple baseline se behtar hai bhi ya nahi.
Step 2 — Data Validation
Duplicate periods, missing results aur incorrect records check karna
Step 3 — Statistical Features
Frequency, recent trend, streaks, transitions aur number distribution
Step 4 — Model & Probability
Baseline probability ke saath candidate predictions compare karna
Step 5 — Validation & Output
Backtest, measured accuracy, probability estimate aur prediction status
